THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES 226
AND 227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA PRAYING TO
DECLARE THAT SECTION 20(2)(A-IV) AND (A-V) OF THE
KARNATAKA CO-OPERATIVE SOCIETIES ACT, 1959 IS
ULTRA VIRES OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA AND ETC.
 THIS WRIT PETITION COMING ON FOR PRELIMINARY
HEARING THIS DAY, THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:
O R D E R
Heard Shri. H.K.Kenchegowda, learned advocate
for the petitioners, Shri. M.Vinodkumar, learned AGA
for respondents no.1, 3 to 6 and 8 and Shri G.V.Shashi
Kumar, learned advocate for respondent No.2.
 2. Shri H.K.Kenchegowda, submits that elections
have already been conducted. Therefore, this petition is
rendered infructuous and accordingly prayed for its

- 304 -
dismissal. He undertakes to file a memo to this effect.
Once received, memo shall be kept in record.
 3. In the circumstances, the petition is
accordingly dismissed as having become infructuous.
 No costs.
Sd/-
